# 常用

## 浏览记录
<a id=浏览记录> </a>
### 基本信息

**Path：** /common/browse

**Method：** GET

**接口描述：**
<p><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">{</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"data"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">{</span><br>

<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"lists"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">[</span><br>

<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">}</span></p>


### 请求参数
**Headers**

| 参数名称  | 参数值  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| Authorization  |   | 是  |  Bearer xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx |  用户Token |
**Query**

| 参数名称  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| page | 是  |   |  页码 |
| page_id | 否  |   |  页码ID  不传查看我浏览的闪页，传入查看浏览闪页的人 |

# 支付

## 发起支付
<a id=发起支付> </a>
### 基本信息

**Path：** /pay/send

**Method：** POST

**接口描述：**
<p><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">{</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"data"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">{</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"prepay_id"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">"wx151741472975500945e6318acba5b10000"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"code_url"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">"weixin://wxpay/bizpayurl?pr=jvthRhjzz"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"order_sn"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">"62d1363824000121"</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">},</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"code"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(181, 206, 168)">200</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"msg"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">""</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"uuid"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">"1ed04224-3bd0-68be-abb0-00155dea7789"</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">}</span></p>


### 请求参数
**Headers**

| 参数名称  | 参数值  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| Content-Type  |  application/x-www-form-urlencoded | 是  |   |   |
| Authorization  |   | 是  |  Bearer xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx |  用户Token |
**Body**

| 参数名称  | 参数类型  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| id | text  |  是 |    |  列表ID |
| type | text  |  是 |    |  支付类型：（已开通：JSAPI，NATIVE） |
| channel | text  |  是 |    |  分销用户渠道ID |

# 关注

## 关注/取消
<a id=关注/取消> </a>
### 基本信息

**Path：** /attention/status

**Method：** POST

**接口描述：**
<p><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">{</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"data"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">{</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"scalar"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(181, 206, 168)">1</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">},</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"code"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(181, 206, 168)">200</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"msg"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">null</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"uuid"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">"1ebe1a0a-e8e5-6e54-bf1a-00155db2a70a"</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">}</span></p>


### 请求参数
**Headers**

| 参数名称  | 参数值  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| Content-Type  |  application/x-www-form-urlencoded | 是  |   |   |
| Authorization  |   | 是  |  Bearer xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx |  用户Token |
**Body**

| 参数名称  | 参数类型  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| id | text  |  是 |    |  ID |
| status | text  |  是 |    |  状态，0取消关注，3用户关注（1，2预留后期自动关注使用） |



### 返回数据

<table>
  <thead class="ant-table-thead">
    <tr>
      <th key=name>名称</th><th key=type>类型</th><th key=required>是否必须</th><th key=default>默认值</th><th key=desc>备注</th><th key=sub>其他信息</th>
    </tr>
  </thead><tbody className="ant-table-tbody"><tr key=0-0><td key=0><span style="padding-left: 0px"><span style="color: #8c8a8a"></span> data</span></td><td key=1><span>object</span></td><td key=2>必须</td><td key=3></td><td key=4><span style="white-space: pre-wrap"></span></td><td key=5></td></tr><tr key=0-0-0><td key=0><span style="padding-left: 20px"><span style="color: #8c8a8a">├─</span> scalar</span></td><td key=1><span>number</span></td><td key=2>必须</td><td key=3></td><td key=4><span style="white-space: pre-wrap"></span></td><td key=5></td></tr><tr key=0-1><td key=0><span style="padding-left: 0px"><span style="color: #8c8a8a"></span> code</span></td><td key=1><span>number</span></td><td key=2>必须</td><td key=3></td><td key=4><span style="white-space: pre-wrap"></span></td><td key=5></td></tr><tr key=0-2><td key=0><span style="padding-left: 0px"><span style="color: #8c8a8a"></span> msg</span></td><td key=1><span>null</span></td><td key=2>必须</td><td key=3></td><td key=4><span style="white-space: pre-wrap"></span></td><td key=5></td></tr><tr key=0-3><td key=0><span style="padding-left: 0px"><span style="color: #8c8a8a"></span> uuid</span></td><td key=1><span>string</span></td><td key=2>必须</td><td key=3></td><td key=4><span style="white-space: pre-wrap"></span></td><td key=5></td></tr>
               </tbody>
              </table>

## 关注列表
<a id=关注列表> </a>
### 基本信息

**Path：** /attention/lists

**Method：** GET

**接口描述：**
<p><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">{</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"data"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">{</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"total"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(181, 206, 168)">1</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"page"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(181, 206, 168)">1</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"size"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(181, 206, 168)">10</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"lists"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">[</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">{</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"id"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(181, 206, 168)">2</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"avatar"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">"<a href="https://cdn.shanwo.cc/local/picture/2021/06/ec76377352f.jpg">https://cdn.shanwo.cc/local/picture/2021/06/ec76377352f.jpg</a>"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"nickname"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">"爱你の小傻瓜"</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">}</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">]</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">},</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"code"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(181, 206, 168)">200</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"msg"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">null</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">,</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">"uuid"</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">:</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&nbsp;</span><span class="colour" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">"1ebe2337-7eee-656a-8624-00155daab927"</span><br>
<span class="colour" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 220)">}</span></p>


### 请求参数
**Headers**

| 参数名称  | 参数值  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| Authorization  |   | 是  |  Bearer xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx |  用户Token |
**Query**

| 参数名称  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| page | 是  |   |  页码 |
| page_id | 否  |   |  闪页id 不传查看我关注的闪页，传入查看关注闪页的人 |

**Headers**

| 参数名称  | 参数值  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |

| Authorization  |   | 是  |  Bearer xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx |  用户Token |
**Body**

| 参数名称  | 参数类型  |  是否必须 | 示例  | 备注  |
| ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| id | text  |  是 |    |   |
| is_open | text  |  否 |    |  是否在广场展示 1展示  0不展示 |
| status | text  |  否 |    |  状态-1:禁用,1:启用 |
